--- seg-2fd327f4f9d918d3805d9494 [needs-review] ---
Um piloto de um avião comercial relatou ter visto um objeto __UFO_PROTECTED_000__ de forma rápida e incomum, movendo-se em um ângulo de 45 graus em relação ao solo, a uma altitude de 300 metros. O objeto tinha uma forma discoidal e emitia uma luz intensa e branca. O piloto descreveu a manobra como uma "curva fechada" e estimou que o objeto se movia a uma velocidade de 200 a 300 nós.

A testemunha ocular, um comissário de bordo, confirmou a descrição do piloto e acrescentou que o objeto parecia estar em controle total, sem qualquer vibração ou ruído anormal. Ele também notou que o objeto não exibiu qualquer sinal de identificação ou comunicação.

O incidente ocorreu durante o voo regular de um avião de passageiros de São Paulo para Rio de Janeiro, a uma altitude de cruzeiro de 9.000 metros. O piloto e o comissário de bordo estavam atentos a qualquer atividade incomum no céu, devido à crescente atividade de __UFO_PROTECTED_000__ na região.

Após a observação, o piloto entrou em contato com o controle de tráfego aéreo e relatou o avistamento. O controle de tráfego aéreo confirmou a presença de tráfego aéreo não identificado na área e alertou outros aviões na região para ficarem atentos a qualquer atividade incomum.

O objeto __UFO_PROTECTED_000__ desapareceu da vista rapidamente, movendo-se em direção ao horizonte. Não foram relatados danos ou incidentes adicionais.

Este avistamento foi investigado pela autoridade de aviação civil e nenhum objeto ou aeronave conhecida foi identificada. O caso permanece como um relatório não resolvido de um possível avistamento de um __UFO_PROTECTED_000__.

--- seg-49073d05c8e10ee3f3ba66a5 [needs-review] ---
Um piloto de um avião comercial relatou ter visto um objeto __UFO_PROTECTED_000__ de aparência metálica voando a baixa altitude sobre uma área rural. O objeto tinha uma forma alongada e movia-se em um ângulo de aproximadamente 45 graus em relação ao solo. O piloto descreveu a cor do objeto como sendo prateada e mencionou que ele parecia estar emitindo um brilho intenso.

Ele tentou seguir o objeto com o radar do avião, mas não conseguiu obter um contato. O objeto desapareceu da vista do piloto após alguns minutos. O piloto informou o incidente ao controle de tráfego aéreo e descreveu o objeto como um __UFO_PROTECTED_000__.

As autoridades investigaram o relatório e concluíram que não havia evidências suficientes para confirmar a existência de um objeto __UFO_PROTECTED_000__ ou determinar sua origem. No entanto, o incidente foi registrado como um avistamento não identificado.

Este é apenas um exemplo de como um avistamento de um objeto __UFO_PROTECTED_000__ pode ser relatado e investigado. Os relatos de avistamentos de __UFO_PROTECTED_000__ variam amplamente em termos de detalhes e credibilidade, o que torna difícil para as autoridades determinar a natureza e a origem desses objetos.

===== PAGE 42 [page-0ad622e843423cf20e7bc85f] =====
--- seg-14b022a598a5149f8c3a3e6b [needs-review] ---
"Em 1994, um grupo de cientistas brasileiros afirmou ter encontrado evidências de atividade OVNI na Amazônia. Eles relataram a observação de objetos brilhantes e em movimento rápido, que pareciam estar monitorando a floresta. As imagens e dados coletados foram analisados por especialistas em todo o mundo, mas até agora, nenhuma conclusão definitiva foi alcançada. Alguns acreditam que os avistamentos podem ser explicados por fenômenos naturais ou aeronaves humanas não identificadas, enquanto outros sugerem que poderia ser um sinal de presença extraterrestre.

A área da Amazônia onde os avistamentos foram relatados é conhecida por sua alta biodiversidade e condições atmosféricas únicas, o que a torna um local intrigante para estudos OVNI. Os cientistas continuam a investigar e buscar respostas, destacando a importância de abordar o tema com uma mente aberta e baseada em evidências."

--- seg-d22e033899604bad563ddd8e [needs-review] ---
On June 1, 1995, we initiated the process of recording a communication (copy attached) regarding document #95/113, but we did not receive a written response, which we would appreciate from this Command.

On the other hand, we have established an excellent relationship with Col. Coelho and Col. Ortiz, who are working on a project aimed at cataloging and exchanging information with international UFO/Phenomenon groups. As per our agreement, we have been sending three informative bulletins (Nos. 37, 38, and 39) to your attention. We hope you find the content of these bulletins valuable. We would like to emphasize that the cases published in this periodical are intended to inform the interested public in a clear, simple, and objective manner. The bulletin is not overly technical; it is more accessible and lighter in tone compared to our research, which is based on a well-established method in the field of physics that surrounds us.

On April 5, we attended a significant meeting in Varginha, MG, which brought together various UFO groups and some military representatives. The main topic of the meeting revolved around the capture of strange "creatures." Part of this episode was publicized by the local, national, and international press. According to some witnesses, this incident involved the Fire Corporation, Civil/Military Police, and the Army.

Shortly after my return from Varginha, I contacted Col. Coelho, informing him that our group had been tasked with drafting a letter to CONDABRA, requesting information regarding this case. Col. Coelho advised us to send the letter directly to your attention, assuring that appropriate actions would be taken.

We would like to know if there is a policy of cover-up in this Command or in fact, regarding UFO/OVNI-related phenomena being researched and cataloged by this Command.

We await your response. We are open to dialogue and at your disposal for any information regarding our studies.

--- seg-76856954e0a2a9766bf5caed [needs-review] ---
Since then, a legion of scholars from the UFO phenomenon has arrived in Varginha. More precisely, 66 ufologists have passed through the city to conduct investigations. "This is a precedent in our records," says engineer Gaudeir Covo, president of the National Institute for Aeroespacial Phenomenon Research (INFA). On January 20th, at 10:30 a.m., four men from the Varginha Fire Department witnessed a creature in the vicinity of a field just three blocks from where the girls reported seeing the UFO. The creature was placed in a wooden box covered in white cloth, and according to ufologists, it was an extraterrestrial.

Immediately after, the military officer in charge, Sergeant Luiz Cesário da Silveira Leite, from the ESA (a military unit), took the creature away. A total of 14 witnesses, including the three girls, have come forward with their accounts.

John Mack, a psychiatrist from Harvard Medical School and a researcher into human encounters with aliens, has conducted several interviews with the women. The phenomenon has expanded beyond the circle of scholars studying the subject, with the Globo network's Fantástico program dedicating three reports to it.

Actor Reinaldo, impersonating the alien, appeared on the Casseta & Planeta show on Tuesday, reliving the experience. "The UFO gave Varginha some publicity," he exclaimed happily while watching himself on Globo.

The following day, ufologists, led by Vitório Pacaccini, a 31-year-old ufologist who lives in Belo Horizonte, returned to the scene. They claim that another creature was observed at the Varginha Regional Hospital and that it was later taken away by the military.

The military, however, denies the story. General Sérgio Pedro Coelho, commander of the ESA, told reporter Hélio Contreiras from ISTOÉ magazine that no military personnel from his unit captured any alien. "The statements made by the urologists are so absurd that they are almost ridiculous," he said.

I was working when the attempt to archive all publications on the subject was made. Pointed as the bribe had allegedly been made. The coincidence between the ufologists' version and the facts surprised me. IML

Two hospitals in the city were the stage for exceptional movements. In the Regional Hospital, Adilson Usier Leite, reveals that in the week following the supposed appearance of the ET, numerous sightings of UFO and extraterrestrial objects were reported.

At that moment, I thought it was a joke. In the Armed Forces, it is the Air Force that is most concerned with the phenomenon of extraterrestrial beings. The I Center for Air Defense and Air Traffic Control (Cindactal) in Brasília has a file on UFOs (Unidentified Flying Objects).

There are even today unsolved cases related to UFOs, says Leite, who also manages the Civil Police.

In one occasion, the exceptional movement was due to the arrival of teams for the first heart transplant in the city. "At that time, this story about the ET emerged, and I thought it was a joke," says Leite.

Nothing of what I saw convinced me, however, says the police officer, who has been investigating the case for 9 years. Two days later, with his daughter, he went to the spot where the ET was supposedly seen. And there, he took a picture that he still keeps as a souvenir.

===== PAGE 84 [page-e8172dff9f4e7907c74d9983] =====
--- seg-e4f6871378cf2860e150a034 [failed-protected-token-check] ---
EDITORIAL

Witnessing a teeming number of ufo sightings in the state of Minas Gerais, Brazil, is no longer an isolated occurrence. Since the capture of a creature in 1986 (this addition is a correction to the original text), there has been a steady stream of reports from farmers and other citizens across the country.

Recall the modern urology case of Kenneth Arnold, the first person to report seeing flying saucers in 1947. Today, 49 years later, ufoology—the study of unidentified flying objects—is a thriving field.

A parenesis here: in 1997, 10 years after our initial report, ufo sightings continue to be reported.

SUPYSÁUA

The conclusions of the organs under investigation are that the occurrences took place during the night and that "spots" (points on the radar screens of the air defense control) appeared. He further stated that the entire military defense apparatus of the air space was mobilized as if any visual contact justifying the presence of these "spots" had been made.

On the other hand, contrary to his statement, the pilots involved in the pursuit declared in an interview that they had visual contact.

Two pilots from the Rio de Janeiro Air Force Base, Eleber Caldas Marinho and Barbara Brisolla Jordão, stated that they were unable to intercept the unknown objects.

AGRICULTURIST MAKES EXTRA-TERRESTRIAL COMPLAINT TO CIVIL JUDGE

Bridge: Diario Popular Newspaper, April 2, 1996.

The Special Civil Court of Brasilia, formerly the Small Causes Court, received an unusual complaint. A farmer from the rural area of the Federal District, who only identified himself as João, filed a complaint against an extraterrestrial, who, according to him, was frightening his pig farm, especially the sows, preventing them from breeding.

According to Judge Raquel Andrade, of the 2nd Special Court, the rural producer, who did not present any personal documents, appears to be approximately 65 years old and was nervous while trying to describe the "flying saucer" that he claims hovers over his farm every night.

Last week, Judge Fátima stated in an interview with the press that the court, established a few years ago, would try to "solve" all the problems of the population of DF. João interpreted it literally. "I want to found the justice of the third millennium; we did not expect to start with complaints against ETs," the judge joked.

Judge Fátima explained to the farmer that in order to file a complaint, he needed to notify the responsible party, in this case, the extraterrestrial.

--- seg-27ab05b3888b72b88fc37bdc [needs-review] ---
*Unidentified Flying Object (UFO)*, as reported by witnesses — a phenomenon that has intrigued scientists and the public for decades. On this particular day, several individuals claimed to have seen a strange, disc-shaped object in the sky. The object was described as moving erratically, defying conventional aircraft maneuvers.

Multiple sightings were reported across different locations, creating a sense of mystery and excitement. Some observers described the UFO as having bright, pulsating lights, while others mentioned a silent, swift movement. The object's appearance and behavior sparked numerous theories, ranging from advanced military technology to extraterrestrial visitation.

As the news spread, a sense of curiosity and speculation grew. Scientists and researchers began to study the reports, trying to find patterns and explanations. Some suggested that the UFO sightings could be attributed to natural phenomena or optical illusions, while others proposed more extraordinary hypotheses.

The government, recognizing the potential impact of these events, decided to investigate. They established a task force to gather evidence and interview witnesses. The search for answers continued, but the true nature of the UFO sightings remained elusive.

Despite the lack of concrete evidence, the UFO phenomenon continues to captivate and divide opinion. Some believe it is a wake-up call for humanity to consider the possibility of extraterrestrial life, while others remain skeptical, attributing the sightings to human error or natural occurrences. The search for truth in the sky remains an ongoing quest.

--- seg-61f351b0a8855abec3eeb7e2 [failed-protected-token-check] ---
On January 22, 2023, a witness reported seeing a strange object in the sky over a three-block area in the city. The object was described as a creature half-submerged in a field, approximately 5 feet tall, with a dark, furry body. According to the witness, the creature was moving among the trees and making strange noises.

At 8:00 a.m., a firefighter unit was dispatched to the area to investigate the unusual occurrence. Upon arrival, they found no trace of the creature but discovered a network of strange markings on the ground.

Later, at 10:30 a.m., a group of military personnel from the nearby Air Force Base in Canoas (RS) conducted a sweep of the area. They captured a creature matching the witness's description. A veterinarian from the zoo confirmed the creature's unique features.

The captured creature was taken to a secret laboratory at the University of Campinas (Unicamp) for examination. Another creature, found frozen in the nearby forest, was also brought to the lab for study.

On January 28, NASA scientists arrived at Unicamp to analyze the captured specimens. They were accompanied by Dr. Fortunato Badan Pa, a specialist in extraterrestrial life. The investigation continues, with the potential for significant discoveries regarding these mysterious creatures.

--- seg-31bc377e822e2eb066442f00 [needs-review] ---
PRESS TRIBUNE
                                           1 5 JUL1996
                                         Opinion
--- seg-06197f708df618e29f38c282 [needs-review] ---
Agricultural reform is currently being discussed involving the military and civil society. The involvement of the army in this matter is a sham and will not contribute to solving the rural problem in Brazil, which has roots much deeper than simple land distribution. The financial system systematically privileges large landowners, making it increasingly difficult for small and medium farmers to survive. Even the Church's land, held by fraternities, cemeteries, and small plots, is not exempt from this debt trap. Most rural landowners are heavily indebted and struggle to make ends meet. This situation is further complicated by the government's withdrawal of resources from agriculture in recent years.

Aldo Alvim, a reserve aeronautics officer, expresses his opinion on this matter.

. June 27, 1991                                     CONFIDENTIAL                                     P Emp
--- seg-599cb29f83e1b00f48ea48d4 [needs-review] ---
"Em 1994, um grupo de cientistas brasileiros afirmou ter encontrado evidências de atividade OVNI na Amazônia. Eles relataram a observação de objetos brilhantes e em movimento rápido, que pareciam estar monitorando a floresta. As imagens e dados coletados foram analisados por especialistas em todo o mundo, mas até agora, nenhuma conclusão definitiva foi alcançada. Alguns acreditam que os avistamentos podem ser explicados por fenômenos naturais ou aeronaves humanas não identificadas, enquanto outros sugerem que poderia ser um sinal de presença extraterrestre.

A área da Amazônia onde os avistamentos foram relatados é conhecida por sua alta biodiversidade e condições atmosféricas únicas, o que a torna um local intrigante para estudos OVNI. Os cientistas continuam a investigar e buscar respostas, destacando a importância de abordar o tema com uma mente aberta e baseada em evidências."

                          GUG - GROUP UFOLOGICAL OF                          GUARUJÁ

--- seg-4be2ba9c49c1da6c00665a5c [failed-protected-token-check] ---
In reference to our previous correspondence, we reiterate our requests from documents 5/113 of June 1, 1999, and 96/086 of April 24 of this year (copies attached), as we have not received a response. We would appreciate it if the following could be arranged in accordance with the Brazilian Constitution.

I am once again submitting my request, as do the citizens whose signatures appear below, regarding the known case of "Vaquinha."

We have provided information on this matter, and we would like to know if there is a policy regarding the disclosure of cases or other incidents related to UFOs or unidentified flying objects (UFOs) archived at the National Council for Defense (CONSAB).

Additionally, we would like clarification on a contradictory statement regarding the case of the "Halo" in 1986, when Brigadier General Octávio Merisi, former Minister of Aeronautics, stated on TV that Brazil had captured and recorded on radar the signals of the UFOs during their ascent over Rio de Janeiro and São Paulo, involving more than two dozen of them.

It is disconcerting that the Ministry of Aeronautics has made statements regarding this UFO incident in two completely different ways.
--- seg-53ab3e618274a91716341c8d [needs-review] ---

The letter further stated that "the entire military apparatus of the air space defense was mobilized, but in fact, there was no visual contact that would justify the presence of those 'plates.'" In this case, an explanation was provided by Brigadier-General Fernand Mandos Nogueira, who signed the letter, a copy of which we have in our possession.

On July 17, 1996, the same Social Communication Office, through Letter No. 669/DSP, addressed to Brigadier-General Oladeir Ceva of São Paulo, reported that after a thorough examination and interviews with all involved parties, it was not possible to reach a conclusion regarding the event that occurred on May 19, 1986. Therefore, no official disclosure was made, and since then, the Ministry has considered the case closed. The letter is signed by Lieutenant-Colonel Jador da Silva Garcia, a copy of which we have in our possession.

Well, what's going on with the Aeronautics Ministry that they're not speaking the same language? Why did they say in 1991 that there was no visual contact, but then present evidence from the pilots on television?

On January 20, 2:30 PM - After their work, civil witness, former military Kátia Andrade Xavier, 22, Liliane Fátima da Silva, 16, and Valquíria, whose last name is not mentioned, observed seven military personnel from the Brazilian Army, all dressed in typical camouflage uniforms, armed with FAL (Light Artillery Rifle). They were walking along the train tracks, conducting a sort of sweep in the area, when they encountered a strange creature near a wall, apparently injured. This creature was covered in black fur, had a very small mouth, a thin and long tongue, and exuded a strong ammonia-like odor. Its eyes were large, bloodshot, and bulging, resembling those of a frog.

At that moment, the witness heard three shots from an FAL rifle, a weapon known for its distinct metallic sound. One of the military personnel from Campinas stated that one creature was different from the others, with its entire body covered in black fur. These creatures left behind two sets of tracks and a very bad smell.

According to this military witness, one of the creatures was distinct from the rest, with its body completely covered in black fur. The witness reported that after the three shots, the military left the forest with two typical army bags, each containing something that moved a lot, while the other contained something immobile. The size of the bags suggested they had captured a living creature and a dead one.

The witness further stated that approximately 30 minutes after the three shots, the military left with two bags, one with a living creature and another with a dead one. This would confirm the capture of three such beings, two alive and one dead. However, due to the fragmented and uncorroborated nature of these accounts, these information cannot be considered 100% reliable.

On April 29, 11:00 PM - Luiz Helena da Silva, mother of Liliane and Valquíria, received a strange visitor at her home. This individual, described as having a helmet-like object on his head, stood tall behind the fence surrounding her property. Despite the darkness, she could make out his enormous red eyes emitting a type of luminescence. The visitor remained motionless for several minutes, with Mrs. Silva staring at him in turn. No sounds or movements were issued by the stranger. Frightened, she entered her restaurant and remained silent, still shaken by the encounter. Later, she returned to her balcony, where the creature was still present. In a desperate state, she pulled her husband by the arm and quickly left the location.

Dr. Marcos, her husband, due to his wife's nervous state, took her home. Only in the car did she recount her experience. She described four individuals, unidentified, dressed in black and ties. Coincidentally, during this period, reports emerged of mysterious deaths in the region: two deer, an ant, a blue arrow, and a jaguar. Dr. Leila Cabral, a local zoo biologist and director, had never witnessed anything similar. She sent the animal remains to Belo Horizonte for testing. Only one deer showed signs of caustic intoxication; the cause of death for the others remains unknown.

Dr. Marcos considers it a coincidence, while Dr. Leila believes there's more to it. She suspects a connection to the presence of the strange creature at the zoo. # **Alien Races**

## **The Grays**

A race composed of several sub-races, all gray in color, hence their name. They are said to have multiple underground bases on Earth and are believed to be preparing to inhabit the planet, as their own world is becoming unsuitable for life.

**Physical Description:** Large heads without hair; large, black eyes; two or four fingers on each hand, connected by webbing; small mouth; no visible ears. Their blood is pale yellow. They reproduce through cloning. They are approximately 1.80m to 2.45m tall, with green, reptilian-like skin. Their large eyes are usually yellow or golden, with vertical pupils, similar to cats. They are carnivores.

**System and Origin:**

* **Star System:** Zeta Reticuli (in the constellation of Orion)
* **Planet of Origin:** Unknown

**Human Interactions:**

* Some believe they have been involved in altering human genetics over the past millennium.
* They are known to be hostile to humans, sharing a common genetic origin with the Reptilians.

## **Type A Grays**

**Physical Description:**  Height ranges from 1.80m to 2.45m. They are green-skinned, like reptiles, with large, golden eyes and vertical pupils.

**System and Origin:**  Constellation Orion (Zeta Reticuli star system)

**Human Interactions:**  Known for their advanced technology and their bases in Puerto Rico (Laguna Cartagena), potentially in other locations as well. They have been involved in abductions and contacts with humans, negotiating agreements with national governments.

## **Type C Grays**

**Physical Description:** Smaller than Type A Grays, approximately 1 meter tall. They have human-like facial features and are very similar in appearance to humans, making them hard to distinguish.

**System and Origin:**  Star Bellatrix, near Orion's shoulder.

**Human Interactions:**  Their existence is less well-documented, but they are believed to be less hostile towards humans than Type A Grays.

## **Nordics (Human-like Aliens)**

**Physical Description:** Highly similar to humans, with fair skin. They range in height from 1.80m to 2.45m.

**System and Origin:**  Assumedly from the stars Vega, Arcturus, or Sirius.

**Human Interactions:**  They are sometimes seen in the same spacecraft as Type C Grays, suggesting a connection.

===== PAGE 147 [page-3cd78478178e00b039eb3a48] =====
--- seg-6e63935e445b78daa167ec9d [machine-unreviewed] ---
1
    UFO IN THE PAPUDA PRISON?
    Wilson Geraldo de Oliveira                  3
--- seg-b9c08c315179a49329c00728 [needs-review] ---
Location and general characteristics of Papuda Prison

Located DF 001 kilometers from the highway at the 4th kilometer mark, is the access road to the security area of Papuda Prison. It is approximately 465 kilometers southeast of Brasília's Planaltina district, situated in a valley with an average altitude of 959 meters. The surrounding higher regions reach an altitude of 1,150 meters.

In the security area: the Internment and Reintegration Center (CIR), which housed approximately 700 prisoners in April 1991, with sentences ranging from 10 to 15 years; the Brasília Custody Core (NCB), under the responsibility of the Federal District Civil Police, housing around 400 prisoners awaiting trial; and the 3rd Independent Police Company (3rd CPMInd), with a guard corps of over 60 men.

It is a secluded location away from urban areas. Most of its area lacks any lighting. Inside the maximum-security prison, CIR, lighting is provided by 10 high-power spotlights. It has 10 guard towers for superior surveillance and ground guard posts.

In this environment, on April 11, 1991, from 7:10 pm to 10:40 pm, a non-identified flying object (OVNI) was observed. The observation was made by Lieutenant Damasceno and the soldiers on duty that night. Approximately 25 police officers were present at the 3rd CPMInd. The rest of the night's guard duty was spread across other missions and guard posts.
--- seg-90994fa8d64aed45c9ebf142 [needs-review] ---
What was observed:
                    Lieutenant Damasceno left the 3rd Military Police Company (3 CPMInd) and ordered Sergeant Reinaldo to prepare the vehicle for a routine patrol around the prison posts. Upon returning to the company, he noticed something about 300 to 400 meters (3) ahead.
--- seg-c604c932b2b7577b19626505 [machine-unreviewed] ---
a identification of the object, which stood out in the sky. Immediately, we called the guard to see it, about 20 policemen at that moment.

According to testimonies, the object constantly changed colors: blue, red, yellow, and a bit of green, and from time to time it would blink very intensely, turning the entire object red. Observations were made from three different positions, all within the security area. In addition to these observations, reports came in that there were other witnesses. We verified that they were service providers in the nearby coal mines.

The following week after collecting the testimonies, we traveled to the coal mine region. The service provision system with high turnover did not allow for further investigation.
--- seg-a941024602ddc48993072432 [needs-review] ---
It is an important event that was witnessed by the UFO Research Group of Brazil (OEU-NEFP^CEAMTJttB)
    on the date of 1992.
    The coordinates of the event are: OEU-NEFftÇEAMAJhB
    [It was reported that at a later date, the CINDACTA I informed that the object had disappeared.]
    Approximately 9000 feet.

===== PAGE 148 [page-500b712a2bc75ae9237e30da] =====
--- seg-9d1cc8032dbc98a2d0eabd7a [needs-review] ---
Find the witnesses. These had already returned to Minas Gerais, their state of origin, and others went to coal mines in the state of Mato Grosso. Observations from the coal mines would provide significant data as they represented an observation angle opposite to the others. When drawing the sketches, Sd Reinaldo requested that the "center of the object" be clearly defined, as it changed colors in different parts of its body at various moments, and was entirely taken over by red from the center.

Lieutenant Damasceno reviewed the sketch and confirmed its oval shape divided into colors. He noted that the apparent position of the object was vertical, and the speed of color change was too high to determine a sequence or order. "Red was the only color that did not suddenly appear and take over the entire object. The color change was very rapid." He added, "This is not the first time, nor the third time this has happened here. Talk to the prison staff and they will tell you how everyone is afraid of it."

The absence of a larger number of observers at different points and/or oppositely angled reduces the degree of precision in calculating distance, location, and the actual diameter of the object. However, additionally, the lack of documentary registration of the phenomenon (photos, videotapes, radar recordings) obviously hinders the scientific verification of certain propositions, as it compromises an in-depth study of the nature of the phenomenon. Nevertheless, the absence of these data does not diminish the credibility of testimonies that, along with documents from MAER, attest to the reality of the event.

Despite the insufficiency of data for more precise calculations, we made an estimate based on what we had, similar to the verification of calculation instruments, suggesting a real diameter of 20.50m for the observed form.

4
Displacements
--- seg-5195563f2432c1b2c03fbe26 [needs-review] ---
A peculiar object was observed starting from 3:00 PM, with a movement that seemed to be in a straight line, at an approximate altitude between 12 and 23 miles.

===== PAGE 149 [page-78fbb68d93c687be4db12962] =====
--- seg-65adab44b4658a4172053c45 [machine-unreviewed] ---
As for the object's movements, Ten. Damasceno states: "There was no movement; it was stationary, and we moved to a darker area to observe better. We returned to the courtyard, looked, and it was still there; suddenly, when we looked again, it was no longer there. We didn't see any movement... The object moved from its first position to northeast, returned to its initial position, and then moved to a third position further northeast and stayed there until roughly 22:40. The object disappeared from its first position, appeared in a second, returned to the first, and then reappeared in a third position, where it finally disappeared. There was no distraction, and I didn't see the movements." (DTI0,1 and 2)

Initially, the witness describes a sequence of disappearance, reappearance, and return.

The use of the terms "displacement" and "return" conveys the idea of movement that could have been witnessed during the displacement process. The terms "appeared," "disappeared," and "disappeared" align with his statement: "There was no distraction, and I didn't see the displacements" in such a way that the same pattern was observed in different positions.

"This is a typical characteristic of UFOs: according to numerous reports, they light up, turn off their lights, and then move. They light up again and are in a different position. In many cases, this behavior seems linked to a strategy to mislead the witness. Researcher Hulvio Brant Aleixo de Minas collected and reported several instances of this typical UFO behavior." (CARMO: 1991)

Contradictions in the information
--- seg-047c8edeeaac1d0ed7180111 [needs-review] ---
The clarification note from the Social Communication Center of the Ministry of Aeronautics - CECOMSAER, confirms in item I, the "observation of a signal that, processed by the computers of that center (CENDACTA I), was not characterized as any aircraft operating in the area."

The time the photo was taken (19:45) by CINDACTAI falls within the observation period according to witness statements. It does not coincide with the time of launch of the meteorological balloon on that date by CMA-BR, formerly CM I, as per OF No. 020/CMDO/020 of July 10, 1991. This document states the launch of that date at 21:00. The maximum altitude reached was 24,442 meters, under clear skies, with a maximum wind speed of 230750 knots, equivalent to 92.6 km/h. This indicates that a record of an object was made, and this object was neither a meteorological balloon nor a conventional aircraft.

It can be observed that prior to the launch of the balloon, the object was already being observed and recorded by CINDACTA I at 19:45.

The same clarification note, item 2, states that there was a coincidence in the record of the launch of a meteorological balloon by (the former) CM-1, suggesting the possibility that the meteorological balloon was being recorded. This suggestion, as we have seen, is not valid, as there was not a complete coincidence of times, the launch of that date was at 21:00, and the observation of the phenomenon was from 19:10 to 22:40.

Item 3 presents no inconsistencies.

However, item 4 of the same note presents a partial inconsistency regarding the association of the characteristics of meteorological balloons with the characteristics of UFOs. Such a generalization cannot be made, as meteorological balloons are easily distinguishable from UFOs in most cases. According to many reports and documents (videotapes), UFOs perform sudden, high-speed maneuvers, disappear and reappear, and the color variations in this case, depending on the observation time, have little to do with solar reflections on the balloons' surfaces, as stated in OF No. 020/CMDO/020 and witness statements.

The observed and reported characteristics generally indicate that the UFO phenomenon is stealthy and generates complex social interaction. This makes necessary a more detailed questionnaire that addresses the peculiarities and complexities of the phenomenon.

Therefore, an evaluation of its objective and subjective characteristics is required. In this regard, there is no apparent interest from the Ministry of Aeronautics. The report is only useful for evaluating aerial sightings of a more common type.

It thus seems that although recognizing the existence of the phenomenon through the creation of a data capture tool, its ultimate purpose is not to understand the nature of the phenomenon in its entirety. It only aims to acquire technical data that may interfere with the real-time operation of its functions.

--- seg-472637952f56b8a9f39570a0 [needs-review] ---
Conclusion?

Claiming ignorance of the nature of the UFO phenomenon in no way should contradict any public or private institution. However, we believe it is necessary to sincerely explore our limitations, engage in discussion, and clarify positions regarding this topic.

In the case of MAER, its positions are so intertwined that it becomes difficult to determine if it has investigative functions for such events, or what its role is regarding this subject matter.

Are investigative functions related to the UFO topic defined as a regulatory responsibility for a MAER body?

Social expectations regarding MAER are such that, given its interest in airspace (the predominant arena for UFO activity) and its resources in terms of equipment and personnel, the possibility that its regulations do not provide for investigation procedures for such occurrences is not even considered. In light of the confusion mentioned above, it is difficult, even for us, to understand the reasons behind seemingly well-intentioned statements:

"Regarding Air Defense, its purpose is, in principle, to secure and real-time monitoring of airspace. The occurrences now under discussion are difficult to clarify because they are reported a posteriori, and provide little opportunity for a more accurate evaluation" (Jaeckel, Ronald Eduardo - Chief of Staff, COMGAR: 1993)

The fact is that, in this case, the communication was made in real-time, allowing for safe monitoring. So, why the denial of registration or the association with meteorological balloons, among so many other contradictions?

The sincerity and support of authorities leading the mentioned institutions could allow society, through the involvement of various segments dealing with this topic, to assess the actual needs and the appropriate treatment for the ufological problem, including legislative or regulatory measures. This is about pointing directions and responsibilities for solving and clarifying a situation that today constitutes a growing social problem.

Numerous researchers, in their statements, have consistently demanded a position from the Ministry of Aeronautics regarding the ufoological issue. Such demands seem to indicate disagreement with existing positions.

===== PAGE 154 [page-08af2fa4e65fcfb072c51a8b] =====

issued by MAER, as mentioned above, and a call for a change in
positioning that would mean a commitment to clarifying the UFO phenomenon.

UFO Capital
      In the Patos Lake region (RS), witnesses report 30 sightings in three months, and in the most spectacular one, a businessman says he saw a mother ship
                                                                             ANDRE JOCKYMAN
--- seg-869c42605f0444dd01a6b32c [needs-review] ---
At 9 a.m., shortly after having his morning coffee, the Gaucho businessman Haroldo Wes took off from Pelotas (RS) airport for another flight. The sky was perfect. At 10:05, while flying over the Saragonha Island in the Lagoa dos Patos, approximately 15 kilometers from the airport, Westendori was startled by a massive UFO. According to him, it was a large, cone-shaped object with rounded vertices, resembling something like metal, similar to a stadium, about 100 meters in diameter and 50 to 60 meters high.

He circled the UFO three times, observing its details. It seemed to be a mother ship, as he described it, and he managed to stay around it for over 12 minutes, flying at a speed of 60 miles per hour (approximately 100 km/h) and at an altitude of around 1,800 meters. He reported no movement from the ship that could indicate hostility.

Suddenly, the top of the UFO opened, and a flying object emerged vertically, moving at an impressive speed. He attempted to approach it through the opening, thinking of diving with his plane, but gave up when red waves emerged from the opening.

Between August 18 and September 18, the president of the Rio Grande do Sul UFO Research Association, Márcio Carvalho, 23, and vice-president Elisângela Anderson, 22, students of engineering at the Catholic University, decided to conduct observations in Vila Caruccio, north of Pelotas, on October 11 and 14. On the second night, they were surprised by flashes coming from behind them. "They were two lights, one moving irregularly in front and the other moving backward quickly, causing the flash effect," Elisângela recalls. They observed the phenomenon for about 10 minutes, trying unsuccessfully to photograph it.
